           │ > Consider a (strongly simplified) booking system at a       │
           │ cashier of a supermarket. The system consists of three       │
           │ processes: the bar code reader BCR, the actual booking       │
           │ program BP, and the printer Printer. The bar code reader     │
           │ reads a bar code and communicates the data of the just       │
           │ scanned product to the booking program. On receiving such    │
           │ data, the booking program transmits the price of the article │
           │ to the printer that prints the article Id together with the  │
           │ price on the receipt. The interactions between the bar code  │
           │ reader and the booking program, and between the booking      │
           │ program and the printer is performed by handshaking. Each    │
           │ process consist of just two states, named 0 and 1. BCR       │
           │ transitions from state 0 to state 1 when a bar code is       │
           │ scanned, and from state 1 to state 0 when the data is sent   │
           │ to BP. BP transitions from state 0 to state 1 when it        │
           │ receives data from BCR, and from state 1 to state 0 when it  │
           │ sends the print command to the printer Printer transitions   │
           │ from state 0 to state 1 when the print code is sent by BP,   │
           │ and from state 1 to state 0 when the article is printed.     │
           │ The complete system is given by: BCR || BP || Printer. Model │
           │ this system. Use the variable names BCR_state, BP_state and  │
           │ Printer_state.                                               │
           │                                                              │

           │                                                              │

           │ program and the printer is performed by handshaking. Each    │
           │ process consist of just two states, named 0 and 1. BCR       │

           │ to BP. BP transitions from state 0 to state 1 when it        │
           │ receives data from BCR, and from state 1 to state 0 when it  │
           │ sends the print command to the printer Printer transitions   │
           │ from state 0 to state 1 when the print code is sent by BP,   │

           │                                                              │
           │ > Consider a (strongly simplified) booking system at a       │
           │ cashier of a supermarket. The system consists of three       │
           │ processes: the bar code reader BCR, the actual booking       │
           │ program BP, and the printer Printer. The bar code reader     │
           │ reads a bar code and communicates the data of the just       │
           │ scanned product to the booking program. On receiving such    │
           │ data, the booking program transmits the price of the article │
           │ to the printer that prints the article Id together with the  │
           │ price on the receipt. The interactions between the bar code  │
           │ reader and the booking program, and between the booking      │
           │ program and the printer is performed by handshaking. Each    │
           │ process consist of just two states, named 0 and 1. BCR       │
           │ transitions from state 0 to state 1 when a bar code is       │
           │ scanned, and from state 1 to state 0 when the data is sent   │
           │ to BP. BP transitions from state 0 to state 1 when it        │
           │ receives data from BCR, and from state 1 to state 0 when it  │
           │ sends the print command to the printer Printer transitions   │
           │ from state 0 to state 1 when the print code is sent by BP,   │
           │ and from state 1 to state 0 when the article is printed.     │
           │ The complete system is given by: BCR || BP || Printer. Model │
           │ this system. Use the variable names BCR_state, BP_state and  │
           │ Printer_state.                                               │
           │                                                              │

           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │

           │         """(Optional) Defines the instances of other modules │
           │ and relates their                                            │
           │         input, output, and shared variables to local         │
           │ variables. Every instance                                    │
           │         variable must be related to a local variable. For    │
           │ example, let M be                                            │
           │         another module with inputs x and y, and output z.    │
           │ The following                                                │
           │         implementation defines an instance of M called m,    │
           │ and connects M's                                             │
           │         input variable x to the local variable self.a, M's   │
           │ input variable y to                                          │
           │         the local variable self.b, and M's output variable z │

           │ cashier of a supermarket. The system consists of three       │

           │ scanned product to the booking program. On receiving such    │
           │ data, the booking program transmits the price of the article │
           │ to the printer that prints the article Id together with the  │
           │ price on the receipt. The interactions between the bar code  │
           │ reader and the booking program, and between the booking      │
           │ program and the printer is performed by handshaking. Each    │
           │ process consist of just two states, named 0 and 1. BCR       │
           │ transitions from state 0 to state 1 when a bar code is       │
           │ scanned, and from state 1 to state 0 when the data is sent   │
           │ to BP. BP transitions from state 0 to state 1 when it        │
           │ receives data from BCR, and from state 1 to state 0 when it  │
           │ sends the print command to the printer Printer transitions   │
           │ from state 0 to state 1 when the print code is sent by BP,   │
           │ and from state 1 to state 0 when the article is printed.     │
           │ The complete system is given by: BCR || BP || Printer. Model │

           │     def types(self):                                         │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the type declarations.         │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines a  │
           │ 8-bit type called T:                                         │
           │         ```                                                  │

           │                                                              │
           │     def locals(self):                                        │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the local variables and their  │
           │ types.                                                       │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an │
           │ 8-bit variable x                                             │
           │         and an integer variable y:                           │

           │                                                              │

           │         """(Optional) Defines the input variables and their  │
           │ types.                                                       │

           │ input variable x,                                            │

           │ Make sure that your code extends the `Module` class below    │

           │                                                              │
           │ > Consider a (strongly simplified) booking system at a       │
           │ cashier of a supermarket. The system consists of three       │
           │ processes: the bar code reader BCR, the actual booking       │
           │ program BP, and the printer Printer. The bar code reader     │
           │ reads a bar code and communicates the data of the just       │
           │ scanned product to the booking program. On receiving such    │
           │ data, the booking program transmits the price of the article │
           │ to the printer that prints the article Id together with the  │
           │ price on the receipt. The interactions between the bar code  │
           │ reader and the booking program, and between the booking      │
           │ program and the printer is performed by handshaking. Each    │
           │ process consist of just two states, named 0 and 1. BCR       │
           │ transitions from state 0 to state 1 when a bar code is       │
           │ scanned, and from state 1 to state 0 when the data is sent   │
           │ to BP. BP transitions from state 0 to state 1 when it        │
           │ receives data from BCR, and from state 1 to state 0 when it  │
           │ sends the print command to the printer Printer transitions   │
           │ from state 0 to state 1 when the print code is sent by BP,   │
           │ and from state 1 to state 0 when the article is printed.     │
           │ The complete system is given by: BCR || BP || Printer. Model │
           │ this system. Use the variable names BCR_state, BP_state and  │
           │ Printer_state.                                               │
           │                                                              │
           │ Reply with your Python code inside one unique code block.    │
           │                                                              │

           │         def outputs(self):                                   │

           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │

           │         ```                                                  │

           │         pass                                                 │

           │     def instances(self):                                     │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the instances of other modules │
           │ and relates their                                            │

           │         variable must be related to a local variable. For    │

           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │

           │                                                              │
           │     def init(self):                                          │
           │         """(Optional) Defines how variables are initialized. │

           │ > Consider a (strongly simplified) booking system at a       │
           │ cashier of a supermarket. The system consists of three       │

           │ scanned product to the booking program. On receiving such    │

           │ price on the receipt. The interactions between the bar code  │
           │ reader and the booking program, and between the booking      │
           │ program and the printer is performed by handshaking. Each    │
           │ process consist of just two states, named 0 and 1. BCR       │
           │ transitions from state 0 to state 1 when a bar code is       │
           │ scanned, and from state 1 to state 0 when the data is sent   │
           │ to BP. BP transitions from state 0 to state 1 when it        │
           │ receives data from BCR, and from state 1 to state 0 when it  │
           │ sends the print command to the printer Printer transitions   │
           │ from state 0 to state 1 when the print code is sent by BP,   │
           │ and from state 1 to state 0 when the article is printed.     │
           │ The complete system is given by: BCR || BP || Printer. Model │

           │                                                              │
           │ BP transitions from state 0 to state 1 when it receives data │
           │ from BCR, and from state 1 to state 0 when it sends the      │
           │ print command to the printer                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │ Printer transitions from state 0 to state 1 when the print   │
           │ code is sent by BP, and from state 1 to state 0 when the     │
           │ article is printed.                                          │
           │                                                              │
           │                                                              │
           │ The complete system is given by:                             │
           │ BCR || BP || Printer.                                        │

           │ Make sure that your code extends the `Module` class below    │
           │ and that it completes the following task.                    │
           │                                                              │
           │ > Consider a (strongly simplified) booking system at a       │
           │ cashier of a supermarket. The system consists of three       │
           │ processes: the bar code reader BCR, the actual booking       │
           │ program BP, and the printer Printer. The bar code reader     │
           │ reads a bar code and communicates the data of the just       │
           │ scanned product to the booking program. On receiving such    │
           │ data, the booking program transmits the price of the article │
           │ to the printer that prints the article Id together with the  │
           │ price on the receipt. The interactions between the bar code  │
           │ reader and the booking program, and between the booking      │
           │ program and the printer is performed by handshaking. Each    │
           │ process consist of just two states, named 0 and 1. BCR       │
           │ transitions from state 0 to state 1 when a bar code is       │
           │ scanned, and from state 1 to state 0 when the data is sent   │
           │ to BP. BP transitions from state 0 to state 1 when it        │
           │ receives data from BCR, and from state 1 to state 0 when it  │
           │ sends the print command to the printer Printer transitions   │
           │ from state 0 to state 1 when the print code is sent by BP,   │
           │ and from state 1 to state 0 when the article is printed.     │
           │ The complete system is given by: BCR || BP || Printer. Model │
           │ this system. Use the variable names BCR_state, BP_state and  │
           │ Printer_state.                                               │
           │                                                              │
           │ Reply with your Python code inside one unique code block.    │
           │                                                              │
           │ ```python                                                    │
           │ class Module:                                                │
           │     """An abstract class to represent a UCLID5 module."""    │
           │                                                              │
           │     def types(self):                                         │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the type declarations.         │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines a  │
           │ 8-bit type called T:                                         │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def types(self):                                     │
           │             self.T = BitVector(8)                            │
           │         ```                                                  │

           │ > Consider a (strongly simplified) booking system at a       │

           │ scanned product to the booking program. On receiving such    │
           │ data, the booking program transmits the price of the article │
           │ to the printer that prints the article Id together with the  │
           │ price on the receipt. The interactions between the bar code  │
           │ reader and the booking program, and between the booking      │
           │ program and the printer is performed by handshaking. Each    │
           │ process consist of just two states, named 0 and 1. BCR       │
           │ transitions from state 0 to state 1 when a bar code is       │
           │ scanned, and from state 1 to state 0 when the data is sent   │
           │ to BP. BP transitions from state 0 to state 1 when it        │
           │ receives data from BCR, and from state 1 to state 0 when it  │
           │ sends the print command to the printer Printer transitions   │
           │ from state 0 to state 1 when the print code is sent by BP,   │
           │ and from state 1 to state 0 when the article is printed.     │
           │ The complete system is given by: BCR || BP || Printer. Model │

           │ > Consider a (strongly simplified) booking system at a       │
           │ cashier of a supermarket. The system consists of three       │
           │ processes: the bar code reader BCR, the actual booking       │
           │ program BP, and the printer Printer. The bar code reader     │
           │ reads a bar code and communicates the data of the just       │
           │ scanned product to the booking program. On receiving such    │
           │ data, the booking program transmits the price of the article │
           │ to the printer that prints the article Id together with the  │
           │ price on the receipt. The interactions between the bar code  │
           │ reader and the booking program, and between the booking      │
           │ program and the printer is performed by handshaking. Each    │
           │ process consist of just two states, named 0 and 1. BCR       │
           │ transitions from state 0 to state 1 when a bar code is       │
           │ scanned, and from state 1 to state 0 when the data is sent   │
           │ to BP. BP transitions from state 0 to state 1 when it        │
           │ receives data from BCR, and from state 1 to state 0 when it  │
           │ sends the print command to the printer Printer transitions   │
           │ from state 0 to state 1 when the print code is sent by BP,   │
           │ and from state 1 to state 0 when the article is printed.     │
           │ The complete system is given by: BCR || BP || Printer. Model │
           │ this system. Use the variable names BCR_state, BP_state and  │

           │ scanned product to the booking program. On receiving such    │
           │ data, the booking program transmits the price of the article │
           │ to the printer that prints the article Id together with the  │
           │ price on the receipt. The interactions between the bar code  │
           │ reader and the booking program, and between the booking      │
           │ program and the printer is performed by handshaking. Each    │
           │ process consist of just two states, named 0 and 1. BCR       │
           │ transitions from state 0 to state 1 when a bar code is       │
           │ scanned, and from state 1 to state 0 when the data is sent   │
           │ to BP. BP transitions from state 0 to state 1 when it        │
           │ receives data from BCR, and from state 1 to state 0 when it  │
           │ sends the print command to the printer Printer transitions   │
           │ from state 0 to state 1 when the print code is sent by BP,   │
           │ and from state 1 to state 0 when the article is printed.     │
           │ The complete system is given by: BCR || BP || Printer. Model │
           │ this system. Use the variable names BCR_state, BP_state and  │

           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def instances(self):                                     │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the instances of other modules │
           │ and relates their                                            │
           │         input, output, and shared variables to local         │
           │ variables. Every instance                                    │
           │         variable must be related to a local variable. For    │
           │ example, let M be                                            │
           │         another module with inputs x and y, and output z.    │
           │ The following                                                │
           │         implementation defines an instance of M called m,    │
           │ and connects M's                                             │
           │         input variable x to the local variable self.a, M's   │
           │ input variable y to                                          │
           │         the local variable self.b, and M's output variable z │
           │ to the local                                                 │
           │         variable self.c:                                     │

           │             self.m = M(x=self.a, y=self.b, z=self.c)         │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │

           │ Make sure that your code extends the `Module` class below    │
           │ and that it completes the following task.                    │
           │                                                              │
           │ > Consider a (strongly simplified) booking system at a       │
           │ cashier of a supermarket. The system consists of three       │
           │ processes: the bar code reader BCR, the actual booking       │
           │ program BP, and the printer Printer. The bar code reader     │
           │ reads a bar code and communicates the data of the just       │
           │ scanned product to the booking program. On receiving such    │
           │ data, the booking program transmits the price of the article │
           │ to the printer that prints the article Id together with the  │
           │ price on the receipt. The interactions between the bar code  │
           │ reader and the booking program, and between the booking      │
           │ program and the printer is performed by handshaking. Each    │
           │ process consist of just two states, named 0 and 1. BCR       │
           │ transitions from state 0 to state 1 when a bar code is       │
           │ scanned, and from state 1 to state 0 when the data is sent   │
           │ to BP. BP transitions from state 0 to state 1 when it        │
           │ receives data from BCR, and from state 1 to state 0 when it  │
           │ sends the print command to the printer Printer transitions   │
           │ from state 0 to state 1 when the print code is sent by BP,   │
           │ and from state 1 to state 0 when the article is printed.     │
           │ The complete system is given by: BCR || BP || Printer. Model │
           │ this system. Use the variable names BCR_state, BP_state and  │
           │ Printer_state.                                               │
           │                                                              │

           │                                                              │
           │ > Consider a (strongly simplified) booking system at a       │
           │ cashier of a supermarket. The system consists of three       │
           │ processes: the bar code reader BCR, the actual booking       │
           │ program BP, and the printer Printer. The bar code reader     │
           │ reads a bar code and communicates the data of the just       │
           │ scanned product to the booking program. On receiving such    │
           │ data, the booking program transmits the price of the article │
           │ to the printer that prints the article Id together with the  │
           │ price on the receipt. The interactions between the bar code  │
           │ reader and the booking program, and between the booking      │
           │ program and the printer is performed by handshaking. Each    │
           │ process consist of just two states, named 0 and 1. BCR       │
           │ transitions from state 0 to state 1 when a bar code is       │
           │ scanned, and from state 1 to state 0 when the data is sent   │
           │ to BP. BP transitions from state 0 to state 1 when it        │
           │ receives data from BCR, and from state 1 to state 0 when it  │
           │ sends the print command to the printer Printer transitions   │
           │ from state 0 to state 1 when the print code is sent by BP,   │
           │ and from state 1 to state 0 when the article is printed.     │
           │ The complete system is given by: BCR || BP || Printer. Model │
           │ this system. Use the variable names BCR_state, BP_state and  │
           │ Printer_state.                                               │
           │                                                              │
           │ Reply with your Python code inside one unique code block.    │
           │                                                              │

           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │

           │ Consider a (strongly simplified) booking system at a cashier │

           │ to BP.                                                       │
           │                                                              │
           │ BP transitions from state 0 to state 1 when it receives data │
           │ from BCR, and from state 1 to state 0 when it sends the      │
           │ print command to the printer                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │ Printer transitions from state 0 to state 1 when the print   │
           │ code is sent by BP, and from state 1 to state 0 when the     │
           │ article is printed.                                          │
           │                                                              │
           │                                                              │

           │ block: specification                                         │
           │ fix: The invariants are implemented incorrectly. The         │
           │ handshake and state transition logic do not reflect the      │
           │ correct interactions of the states. Specifically, invariants │
           │ such as `bcr_to_bp_handshake_inv` and                        │
